	develop_102_coal_deposits = {

		icon = coal

		allowed = {

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 102
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation4
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 2
			owns_state = 102
			controls_state = 102
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 102
			controls_state = 102
			102 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_102_coal_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 59
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = coal
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			102 = { set_state_flag = has_102_templateResource_developed }
			102 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = coal
					amount = 14
				}
			}
		}
	}

				}
			}
		}

		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 3
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = coal
					amount < 1
				}
			}

		remove_effect = {
			297 = { set_state_flag = has_297_templateResource_developed }

					amount = 5

		icon = steel

		allowed = {

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 22
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation2
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 2
			owns_state = 22
			controls_state = 22
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 22
			controls_state = 22
			22 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_22_steel_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 38
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = steel
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			22 = { set_state_flag = has_22_templateResource_developed }
			22 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = steel
					amount = 9
				}
			}
		}
	}

		icon = chromium

		allowed = {

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 87
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation3
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 2
			owns_state = 87
			controls_state = 87
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 87
			controls_state = 87
			87 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_87_chromium_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 49
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = chromium
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			87 = { set_state_flag = has_87_templateResource_developed }
			87 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = chromium
					amount = 12
				}
			}
		}
	}
